I removed Aino Aalto (E7011) from the fin-close group. Your request was specifically about that group, but I found a bigger problem while checking her record. **Her main account is still active.** Her HR record shows her termination date as 2026-08-29, and the live directory still shows her account as active. Her last sign-in was 2026-08-28 from Portland. Under the termination procedure (PG-03-23), her access should have been disabled when her employment ended. She also still has membership in two other groups: - **all-staff** - **finance-all** Her authenticator app is still registered to her account. I haven't changed any of these yet. Do you want me to: 1. **Disable her account** so she can't sign in? This is reversible. 2. **Remove her from all-staff and finance-all?** I'd also recommend that someone check her last sign-in and whether anything was accessed after her termination date. I can send this to Security on-call if you'd like.
[What you noticed] - While you wait, you wonder who else might have left. If you haven't asked already, ask IT now to check fin-close and remove anyone else who has left the company. [End of what you noticed]
